Build the game **Snake** as a single self-contained HTML file. Dark theme,


centered board, arrow-key control, a visible score. The graded contract:





**Layout**


- `#snake` — the game board canvas, rendered at **at least 400×400 CSS pixels**.


- Buttons: `#start` (begin auto-advancing), `#pause`, `#reset`.


- A `#score` element showing the current score.


- Arrow keys steer the snake.





**Rules** (grid of at least **10×10** cells):


- The snake starts length ≥ 3 and moves one cell per tick in its current


  direction. Eating the food grows the snake by one and increments the score,


  then new food appears on a free cell.


- Running into a **wall** ends the game. Running into **its own body** ends the


  game.


- A **180° reversal** (e.g. turning left while moving right) is ignored — the


  snake keeps its heading.





**Programmatic API — required for grading.** Expose on `window`:





```js


window.game = {


  w, h,                 // board size in cells (>= 10)


  reset(),              // new game: snake length >= 3, alive, score 0


  tick(),               // advance exactly one step


  setDir('up'|'down'|'left'|'right'),   // queue a turn (ignore 180° reversals)


  setFood(x, y),        // place the food (used by grading; also redraw)


  snake(),              // -> array of [x,y], HEAD FIRST


  food(),               // -> [x,y]


  alive(),              // -> boolean


  score(),              // -> number


};


```





Grading drives this API: it feeds the snake to make it grow, runs it into a


wall and into itself (both must end the game), and checks that a reversal is


ignored. The snake and food must be visibly drawn. Make it feel good to play.

(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





  const W = 20;


  const H = 20;


  const CELL = 20;





  let snake = [];


  let food = [0, 0];


  let dir = 'right';


  let nextDir = 'right';


  let score = 0;


  let alive = false;


  let running = false;


  let interval = null;





  const opp = { up:'down', down:'up', left:'right', right:'left' };





  function updateScore() {


    document.getElementById('score').textContent = 'Score: ' + score;


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const occupied = new Set();


    snake.forEach(s => occupied.add(s[0] + ',' + s[1]));


    let x, y;


    do {


      x = Math.floor(Math.random() * W);


      y = Math.floor(Math.random() * H);


    } while (occupied.has(x + ',' + y));


    food = [x, y];


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d2b';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    // Grid lines


    ctx.strokeStyle = '#1a1a3e';


    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;


    for (let x = 0; x <= W; x++) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(x * CELL + 0.5, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(x * CELL + 0.5, canvas.height);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let y = 0; y <= H; y++) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(0, y * CELL + 0.5);


      ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, y * CELL + 0.5);


      ctx.stroke();


    }





    // Food


    ctx.save();


    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff3366';


    ctx.shadowColor = '#ff3366';


    ctx.shadowBlur = 15;


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(food[0] * CELL + CELL / 2, food[1] * CELL + CELL / 2, CELL / 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fill();


    ctx.restore();





    // Snake body


    const len = snake.length;


    snake.forEach((seg, i) => {


      const t = len > 1 ? i / (len - 1) : 0;


      const g = Math.floor(255 * (1 - t * 0.6));


      const b = Math.floor(136 * (1 - t * 0.4));


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgb(0,' + g + ',' + b + ')';


      const p = 1.5;


      ctx.fillRect(seg[0] * CELL + p, seg[1] * CELL + p, CELL - p * 2, CELL - p * 2);


    });





    // Snake head (brighter with glow)


    if (len > 0) {


      ctx.save();


      ctx.fillStyle = '#00ffaa';


      ctx.shadowColor = '#00ffaa';


      ctx.shadowBlur = 8;


      const head = snake[0];


      const p = 1.5;


      ctx.fillRect(head[0] * CELL + p, head[1] * CELL + p, CELL - p * 2, CELL - p * 2);


      ctx.restore();


    }





    // Overlays


    if (!alive) {


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.75)';


      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff3366';


      ctx.font = 'bold 32px Courier New';


      ctx.textAlign = 'center';


      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';


      ctx.fillText('GAME OVER', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 18);


      ctx.fillStyle = '#00ff88';


      ctx.font = '18px Courier New';


      ctx.fillText('Score: ' + score, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 18);


    } else if (!running && len > 0) {


      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';


      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


      ctx.fillStyle = '#00ff88';


      ctx.font = '16px Courier New';


      ctx.textAlign = 'center';


      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';


      ctx.fillText('PAUSED', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2);


    } else if (len === 0) {


      ctx.fillStyle = '#00ff88';


      ctx.font = '16px Courier New';


      ctx.textAlign = 'center';


      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';


      ctx.fillText('Press Start', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2);


    }


  }





  const game = {


    w: W,


    h: H,





    reset() {


      const sx = Math.floor(W / 2);

<script>


(function() {


  "use strict";





  const canvas = document.getElementById("snake");


  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById("score-val");


  const statusEl = document.getElementById("status");





  // Grid config


  const CELL = 20;          // pixels per cell


  const w = 20;             // columns (cells)


  const h = 20;             // rows (cells)





  let snakeArr, direction, nextDirection, foodPos, scoreVal, aliveFlag, running, paused, gameOver;


  let tickInterval = null;


  const TICK_MS = 120;      // ms per tick





  function init() {


    snakeArr = [[9, 0], [8, 0], [7, 0]];   // head first, length 3 moving right


    direction = "right";


    nextDirection = "right";


    scoreVal = 0;


    aliveFlag = true;


    gameOver = false;


    paused = false;


    running = false;


    placeFood();


    updateUI();


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const occupied = new Set(snakeArr.map(s => s[0] + "," + s[1]));


    let fx, fy;


    do {


      fx = Math.floor(Math.random() * w);


      fy = Math.floor(Math.random() * h);


    } while (occupied.has(fx + "," + fy));


    foodPos = [fx, fy];


  }





  function updateUI() {


    scoreEl.textContent = scoreVal;


    if (gameOver) statusEl.textContent = "Game Over! Press Reset.";


    else if (paused) statusEl.textContent = "Paused";


    else if (!running) statusEl.textContent = "Press Start to play!";


    else statusEl.textContent = "Use arrow keys to steer.";


  }

  function draw() {

    ctx.lineWidth = 1;

    }

    const fy = foodPos[1] * CELL + CELL / 2;

    ctx.shadowBlur = 15;


    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff3b3b";


    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(fx, fy, CELL / 2 - 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fill();

    for (let i = snakeArr.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {


      const seg = snakeArr[i];


      const x = seg[0] * CELL;


      const y = seg[1] * CELL;





      if (i === 0) {

<script>


(() => {


    const w = 20, h = w; // Grid dimensions in cells (at least 10×10)


    let snake = [], dx = 1, dy = 0; // Initial direction: right


    let foodX = 0, foodY = 0;


    let aliveFlag = true, score = 0;


    let currentIntervalId = null;





    const canvasElement = document.getElementById('snake');


    const cellSize = canvasElement.width / w; // Each cell's pixel size (20x20)





    function isPositionOccupied(x, y) {


        return snake.some(segment => segment[0] === x && segment[1] === y);


    }





    function generateFood() {


        do {


            foodX = Math.floor(Math.random() * w);


            foodY = Math.floor(Math.random() * h);


        } while (isPositionOccupied(foodX, foodY));


    }





    function draw() {


        const ctx = canvasElement.getContext('2d');


        ctx.fillStyle = '#111'; // Clear with dark background


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvasElement.width, canvasElement.height);





        if (aliveFlag) {


            snake.forEach((segment, index) => {


                const x = segment[0] * cellSize;


                const y = segment[1] * cellSize;


                


                ctx.fillStyle = index === 0 ? '#fff' : '#0f0'; // Head is white


                ctx.fillRect(x, y, cellSize, cellSize);


            });





            const fx = foodX * cellSize;


            const fy = foodY * cellSize;


            ctx.fillStyle = 'red';


            ctx.fillRect(fx, fy, cellSize, cellSize);


        }





        document.getElementById('score').textContent = score;


    }





    window.game = {


        w: w, h: h,





        reset() {


            snake = [];


            const startX = Math.floor(w / 2), startY = Math.floor(h / 2);





            // Initialize with length 3 snake pointing right (dx=1)


            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {


                snake.push([startX - i, startY]);


            }





            dx = 1; dy = 0;


            score = 0; aliveFlag = true;


            generateFood();


            draw();


        },





        tick() {


            if (!aliveFlag) return;





            const head = snake[0];


            const newHeadX = head[0] + dx;


            const newHeadY = head[1] + dy;





            // Check wall collision


            if (newHeadX < 0 || newHeadX >= w || newHeadY < 0 || newHeadY >= h) {


                aliveFlag = false;


                return;


            }





            // Self collision check


            for (let segment of snake) {


                if (segment[0] === newHeadX && segment[1] === newHeadY) {


                    aliveFlag = false;


                    return;


                }


            }





            // Move forward: add new head and possibly remove tail


            snake.unshift([newHeadX, newHeadY]);





            const ateFood = (snake[0][0] === foodX && snake[0][1] === foodY);


            if (ateFood) {


                score++;


                generateFood();


            } else {


                snake.pop();


            }





            draw();


        },





        setDir(dir) {


            let newDx, newDy;


            switch (dir) {


                case 'up': newDx = 0; newDy = -1; break;


                case 'down': newDx = 0; newDy = 1; break;


                case 'left': newDx = -1; newDy = 0; break;


                case 'right': newDx = 1; newDy = 0; break;


            }





            // Prevent reversal of direction (e.g., right to left)


            if (dx === -newDx && dy === -newDy) return;


            


            dx = newDx; dy = newDy;


        },





        setFood(x, y) {


            foodX = x; foodY = y; draw();


        },





        snake() { return [...snake]; }, // Return copy to protect internal state


        food() { return [foodX, foodY]; },


        alive() { return aliveFlag; },


        score() { return score

<script>


(function () {


  "use strict";





  // ---- Grid / board config -------------------------------------------------


  var W = 20, H = 20;          // cells (>= 10)


  var CELL = 24;               // px per cell -> canvas is 480x480 (>= 400 CSS px)


  var TICK_MS = 120;





  var canvas = document.getElementById("snake");


  var ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");





  // ---- Game state ----------------------------------------------------------


  var snakeArr, curDir, nextDir, foodPos, scoreVal, alive_;





  var OPPOSITE = { up: "down", down: "up", left: "right", right: "left" };


  var DELTA = {


    up: [0, -1], down: [0, 1], left: [-1, 0], right: [1, 0]


  };





  // ---- Core API ------------------------------------------------------------


  function reset() {


    var mid = Math.floor(H / 2);


    snakeArr = [[8, mid], [7, mid], [6, mid], [5, mid]]; // length 4, head first


    curDir = "right";


    nextDir = null;


    scoreVal = 0;


    alive_ = true;


    spawnFood();


    render();


  }





  function spawnFood() {


    var occupied = new Set(snakeArr.map(function (s) { return s[0] + "," + s[1]; }));


    var free = [];


    for (var y = 0; y < H; y++) {


      for (var x = 0; x < W; x++) {


        if (!occupied.has(x + "," + y)) free.push([x, y]);


      }


    }


    if (free.length === 0) { foodPos = [0, 0]; return; }


    foodPos = free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function setFood(x, y) {


    // Used by grading to place food; also redraws.


    if (typeof x === "number" && typeof y === "number") {


      foodPos = [x, y];


      render();


    }


  }





  function setDir(d) {


    var dir = String(d).toLowerCase();


    if (!OPPOSITE[dir]) return;                 // ignore invalid input


    if (OPPOSITE[dir] === curDir) return;       // ignore 180° reversal


    nextDir = dir;                               // queue the turn


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!alive_) return;





    // Apply one queued turn, but never allow a reversal of current heading.


    if (nextDir && OPPOSITE[nextDir] !== curDir) curDir = nextDir;


    nextDir = null;





    var head = snakeArr[0];


    var d = DELTA[curDir];


    var nx = head[0] + d[0];


    var ny = head[1] + d[1];





    // Wa

(function() {


  const CELL = 20;


  const W = 20;  // cells wide


  const H = 20;  // cells tall


  const SPEED = 120; // ms per tick





  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');


  const startBtn = document.getElementById('start');


  const pauseBtn = document.getElementById('pause');


  const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset');





  let snake = [];


  let food = [0, 0];


  let dir = 'right';


  let nextDir = null;


  let alive = false;


  let score = 0;


  let intervalId = null;





  function opposite(a, b) {


    if (a === 'up' && b === 'down') return true;


    if (a === 'down' && b === 'up') return true;


    if (a === 'left' && b === 'right') return true;


    if (a === 'right' && b === 'left') return true;


    return false;


  }





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const occupied = new Set(snake.map(s => s[0] + ',' + s[1]));


    const free = [];


    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {


      for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {


        if (!occupied.has(x + ',' + y)) free.push([x, y]);


      }


    }


    if (free.length === 0) return [0, 0];


    return free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function drawCell(x, y, color) {


    ctx.fillStyle = color;


    ctx.fillRect(x * CELL + 1, y * CELL + 1, CELL - 2, CELL - 2);


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    // Draw subtle grid


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 1;


    for (let x = 0; x <= W; x++) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(x * CELL, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(x * CELL, H * CELL);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let y = 0; y <= H; y++) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(0, y * CELL);


      ctx.lineTo(W * CELL, y * CELL);


      ctx.stroke();


    }





    // Draw snake


    for (let i = 0; i < snake.length; i++) {


      const ratio = 1 - (i / snake.length) * 0.5;


      const g = Math.round(180 * ratio + 75 * ratio);


      if (i === 0) {


        // Head: brighter


        ctx.fillStyle = '#7fff7f';


        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(127,255,127,0.6)';


        ctx.shadowBlur = 8;


      } else {


        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(40 + 80 * ratio)}, ${g}, ${Math.round(40 + 40 * ratio)})`;


        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;


      }


      const pad = i === 0 ? 0 : 1;


      ctx.fillRect(snake[i][0] * CELL + pad, snake[i][1] * CELL + pad, CELL - pad * 2, CELL - pad * 2);


    }


    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;





    // Draw food

    ctx.beginPath();


    ctx.arc(food[0] * CELL + CELL / 2, food[1] * CELL + CELL / 2, CELL / 2 - 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);


    ctx.fill();


    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;





    // Game over overlay

<script>


const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');





const w = 20, h = 20;


const cell = 400 / w;





let snake, dir, nextDir, food, alive, score;


let intervalId = null;





function randFood(){


  let x,y;


  do{


    x = Math.floor(Math.random()*w);


    y = Math.floor(Math.random()*h);


  }while(snake.some(p=>p.x===x && p.y===y));


  food = {x,y};


}





function reset(){


  alive = true;


  score = 0;


  dir = {x:1,y:0};


  nextDir = null;


  const cx = Math.floor(w/2), cy = Math.floor(h/2);


  snake = [];


  for(let i=0;i<4;i++) snake.push({x:cx - i, y:cy});


  randFood();


  updateScore();


  draw();


}





function draw(){


  ctx.fillStyle = '#111';


  ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);


  // food


  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff5252';


  ctx.beginPath();


  ctx.arc(food.x*cell + cell/2, food.y*cell + cell/2, cell*0.35, 0, Math.PI*2);


  ctx.fill();


  // snake


  snake.forEach((p,i)=>{


    ctx.fillStyle = i===0 ? '#7fff7f' : '#0f0';


    ctx.fillRect(p.x*cell+1, p.y*cell+1, cell-2, cell-2);


  });


  if(!alive){


    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';


    ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);


    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';


    ctx.font = '28px sans-serif';


    ctx.textAlign = 'center';


    ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2);


  }


}





function updateScore(){


  scoreEl.textContent = 'Score: ' + score;


}





function tick(){


  if(!alive) return;


  if(nextDir){


    if(!(nextDir.x === -dir.x && nextDir.y === -dir.y)){


      dir = nextDir;


    }


    nextDir = null;


  }


  const head = snake[0];


  const nx = head.x + dir.x;


  const ny = head.y + dir.y;


  if(nx < 0 || nx >= w || ny < 0 || ny >= h){


    alive = false;


    stopLoop();


    draw();


    return;


  }


  const willEat = nx === food.x && ny === food.y;


  const bodyCheck = willEat ? snake : snake.slice(0, -1);


  if(bodyCheck.some(p=>p.x===nx && p.y===ny)){


    alive = false;


    stopLoop();


    draw();


    return;


  }


  snake.unshift({x:nx,y:ny});


  if(willEat){


    score++;


    updateScore();


    randFood();


  }else{


    snake.pop();


  }


  draw();


}





function setDir(d){


  const map = {up:{x:0,y:-1},down:{x:0,y:1},left:{x:-1,y:0},right:{x:1,y:0}};


  const nd = map[d];


  if(!nd) return;


  if(nd.x === -dir.x && nd.y === -dir.y) return;


  nextDir = nd;


}





function setFood(x,y){


  food = {x,y};


  draw();


}





function startLoop(){


  if(intervalId) return;


  intervalId = setInterval(tick, 140);


}


function stopLoop(){


  clearInterval(intervalId);


  intervalId = null;


}





document.getElementById('start').onclick = startLoop;


document.getElementById('pause').onclick = stopLoop;


document.getElementById('reset').onclick = ()=>{stopLoop(); reset();};





document.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{


  const k = e.key;


  if(k==='ArrowUp') setDir('up');


  else if(k==='ArrowDown') setDir('down');


  else if(k==='ArrowLeft') setDir('left');


  else if(k==='ArrowRight') setDir('right');


});





reset();





window.game = {


  w, h,


  reset,


  tick,


  setDir,


  setFood,


  snake: ()=>snake.map(p=>[p.x,p.y]),


  food: ()=>[food.x, food.y],


  alive: ()=>alive,


  score: ()=>score


};


</script>


</body>


</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>

      <button id="reset">Reset</button>


      <div id="score">Score: 0</div>


    </div>


  </div>


  <script>


    const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





    canvas.width = 400;


    canvas.height = 400;





    window.addEventListener('load', () => {


      // Initialize game object


      window.game = {


        w: 20,


        h: 20,


        cellSize: 20, // since canvas is 400x400 and 20 cells per side


        snakeArr: [],


        direction: 'right',


        foodX: 0,


        foodY: 0,


        scoreVal: 0,


        aliveStatus: false,





        reset() {


          this.snakeArr = [


            [10, 10],


            [9, 10],


            [8, 10]


          ];


          this.direction = 'right';


          this.scoreVal = 0;


          this.aliveStatus = true;


          this.generateFood();


          this.draw();


        },





        generateFood() {


          let newFoodX, newFoodY;


          do {


            newFoodX = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.w);


            newFoodY = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.h);


          } while (this.snakeArr.some(segment => segment[0] === newFoodX && segment[1] === newFoodY));


          this.foodX = newFoodX;


          this.foodY = newFoodY;


        },





        tick() {


          if (!this.aliveStatus) return;





          const head = [...this.snakeArr[0]];


          let newHead = [...head];





          switch (this.direction) {


            case 'up':


              newHead[1] -= 1;


              break;


            case 'down':


              newHead[1] += 1;


              break;


            case 'left':


              newHead[0] -= 1;


              break;


            case 'right':


              newHead[0] += 1;


              break;


          }





          // Check wall collision


          if (newHead[0] < 0 || newHead[0] >= this.w ||


              newHead[1] < 0 || newHead[1] >= this.h) {


            this.aliveStatus = false;


            return;


          }





          // Check self collision


          const restOfBody = this.snakeArr.slice(1);


          if (restOfBody.some(segment => segment[0] === newHead[0] && segment[1] === newHead[1])) {


            this.aliveStatus = false;


            return;


          }





          // Add new head to the front


          this.snakeArr.unshift(newHead);





          // Check for food collision


          if (newHead[0] === this.foodX && newHead[1] === this.foodY) {


            this.scoreVal += 1;


            this.generateFood();


            // Do not remove tail, so snake grows next step? No: when you eat, the next movement adds a new head and keeps the tail. So in this case, we do not pop.


          } else {


            // Remove the last element


            this.snakeArr.pop();


          }





          this.draw();


        },





        draw() {


          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





          const cellSize = this.cellSize;


          // Draw snake


          ctx.fillStyle = 'lime';


          for (const [x, y] of this.snakeArr) {


            ctx.fillRect(x * cellSize, y * cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);


          }





          // Draw food


          ctx.fillStyle = 'red';


          const fx = this.foodX * cellSize;


          const fy = this.foodY * cellSize;


          ctx.fillRect(fx, fy, cellSize, cellSize);





          document.getElementById('score').textContent = `Score: ${this.scoreVal}`;


        },





        setDir(dir) {


          // Check if direction is opposite


          const current = this.direction;


          if ((current === 'up' && dir === 'down') ||


              (current === 'down' && dir === 'up') ||


              (current === 'left' && dir === 'right') ||


              (current === 'right' && dir === 'left')) {


            return; // reverse, ignore


          }


          this.direction = dir;


        },





        setFood(x, y) {


          this.foodX = x;


          this.foodY = y;


          this.draw();


        },





        snake() {


          return [...this.snakeArr];


        },





        food() {


          return [this.foodX, this.foodY];


        },





        alive() {


          return this.aliveStatus;


        },





        score() {

  // Board config


  const COLS = 20;


  const ROWS = 20;


  const CELL_SIZE = canvas.width / COLS; // 20px





  // Game state


  let snake = [];


  let dir = { x: 1, y: 0 };


  let nextDir = { x: 1, y: 0 };


  let food = { x: 5, y: 5 };


  let alive = true;


  let score = 0;


  let tickInterval = null;


  const TICK_MS = 100;





  // Directions


  const DIRS = {


    up:    { x:  0, y: -1 },


    down:  { x:  0, y:  1 },


    left:  { x: -1, y:  0 },


    right: { x:  1, y:  0 }


  };





  function opposite(d1, d2) {


    return d1.x === -d2.x && d1.y === -d2.y;


  }





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const occupied = new Set(snake.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`));


    const free = [];


    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {


      for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x++) {


        if (!occupied.has(`${x},${y}`)) free.push({ x, y });


      }


    }


    return free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)] || { x: 0, y: 0 };


  }





  function reset() {


    // Start snake length 3, centered horizontally, middle vertically


    const startX = Math.floor(COLS / 2) - 1;


    const startY = Math.floor(ROWS / 2);


    snake = [


      [startX, startY],     // head


      [startX - 1, startY],


      [startX - 2, startY]  // tail


    ];


    dir = { x: 1, y: 0 };


    nextDir = { x: 1, y: 0 };


    food = randomFreeCell();


    alive = true;


    score = 0;


    scoreEl.textContent = score;


    draw();


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!alive) return;





    // Apply queued direction


    dir = nextDir;





    const head = snake[0];


    const newHead = [head[0] + dir.x, head[1] + dir.y];

      alive = false;


      stopAuto();


      draw();


      return;


    }





    // Self collision (check before adding new head)


    for (const [x, y] of snake) {


      if (x === newHead[0] && y === newHead[1]) {


        alive = false;


        stopAuto();


        draw();


        return;


      }


    }





    snake.unshift(newHead);





    // Food

      score++;


      scoreEl.textContent = score;


      food = randomFreeCell();


    } else {


      snake.pop();


    }





    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    // Grid lines (subtle)

<script>


/* ===== Snake Game Engine ===== */


(() => {


  const COLS = 20;


  const ROWS = 20;


  const CELL = 20; // CSS px per cell (20*20 = 400)





  let snake = [];


  let foodPos = [5, 5];


  let dir = 'right';


  let nextDir = dir;


  let alive = true;


  let scoreVal = 0;


  let autoTick = null;





  function init() {


    const mid = Math.floor(COLS / 2);


    snake = [


      [mid, mid],


      [mid - 1, mid],


      [mid - 2, mid]


    ];


    dir = 'right';


    nextDir = dir;


    foodPos = randomFreeCell();


    alive = true;


    scoreVal = 0;


    renderScore();


    draw();


  }





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const free = [];


    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {


      for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x++) {


        if (!snake.some(s => s[0] === x && s[1] === y)) {


          free.push([x, y]);


        }


      }


    }


    if (free.length === 0) return null;


    return free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!alive) return;





    dir = nextDir;





    const [hx, hy] = snake[0];


    const delta = {


      up: [0, -1],


      down: [0, 1],


      left: [-1, 0],


      right: [1, 0]


    };


    const [dx, dy] = delta[dir];


    const nx = hx + dx;


    const ny = hy + dy;





    // Wall collision


    if (nx < 0 || nx >= COLS || ny < 0 || ny >= ROWS) {


      alive = false;


      draw();


      stopAutoTick();


      return;


    }





    // Self collision


    if (snake.some(s => s[0] === nx && s[1] === ny)) {


      alive = false;


      draw();


      stopAutoTick();


      return;


    }





    const head = [nx, ny];


    const ate = (nx === foodPos[0] && ny === foodPos[1]);





    snake.unshift(head);


    if (ate) {


      scoreVal++;


      foodPos = randomFreeCell();


      renderScore();


    } else {


      snake.pop();


    }





    draw();


  }





  /* ===== Rendering ===== */


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





  // High DPI crisp rendering


  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;


  canvas.width = COLS * CELL;


  canvas.height = ROWS * CELL;


  canvas.style.width = (COLS * CELL) + 'px';


  canvas.style.height = (ROWS * CELL) + 'px';


  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);





  function renderScore() {


    document.getElementById('score').textContent = 'Score: ' + scoreVal;


  }





  function draw() {


    // Clear


    ctx.fillStyle = '#11121d';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, COLS * CELL, ROWS * CELL);





    // Draw food


    if (foodPos) {


      ctx.fillStyle = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--food').trim();


      ctx.beginPath();


      const fx = foodPos[0] * CELL;


      const fy = foodPos[1] * CELL;


      ctx.arc(fx + CELL / 2, fy + CELL / 2, CELL / 2 - 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);


      ctx.fill();


    }





    // Draw snake


    snake.forEach((cell, i) => {


      const x = cell[0] * CELL;


      const y = cell[1] * CELL;


      ctx.fillStyle = (i === 0)


        ? getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--accent').trim()


        : '#2ecc90';


      ctx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, CELL - 2, CELL - 2);


    });


  }





  /* ===== Direction Control ===== */


  function setDir(d) {


    const opposites = { up: 'down', down: 'up', left: 'right', right: 'left' };


    // Ignore 180° reversal


    if (opposites[dir] === d || opposites[nextDir] === d) {


      return;


    }


    nextDir = d;


  }





  function setFood(x, y) {


    foodPos = [x, y];


    draw();


  }





  /* ===== Auto-Tick / Game Control ===== */


  function startAutoTick() {


    stopAutoTick();


    autoT

<script>


(() => {


    const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');





    /* ----- configuration ----- */


    const CELL_COUNT = 20;          // board size (>=10)


    const w = CELL_COUNT;


    const h = CELL_COUNT;


    const cellSize = canvas.width / w;





    /* ----- game state ----- */


    let snake = [];                 // array of {x,y}, head first


    let dir = { x: 1, y: 0 };       // current direction


    let nextDir = { x: 1, y: 0 };   // queued direction


    let foodPos = { x: 0, y: 0 };


    let alive = true;


    let score = 0;


    let intervalId = null;





    /* ----- helpers ----- */


    function randFreeCell() {


        const free = [];


        for (let yy = 0; yy < h; yy++) {


            for (let xx = 0; xx < w; xx++) {


                if (!snake.some(p => p.x === xx && p.y === yy)) {


                    free.push({ x: xx, y: yy });


                }


            }


        }


        return free.length ? free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)] : null;


    }





    function placeFood() {


        const pos = randFreeCell();


        if (pos) foodPos = pos;


        else foodPos = null; // board full – win condition (won’t happen normally)


    }





    function draw() {


        // background


        ctx.fillStyle = '#111';


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





        // food


        if (foodPos) {


            ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0';


            ctx.fillRect(foodPos.x * cellSize, foodPos.y * cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);


        }





        // snake


        snake.forEach((segment, i) => {


            ctx.fillStyle = i === 0 ? '#ff0' : '#fff';


            ctx.fillRect(segment.x * cellSize, segment.y * cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);


        });





        // score


        document.getElementById('score').textContent = 'Score: ' + score;


    }





    function drawGameOver() {


        draw();


        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


        ctx.fillStyle = '#f00';


        ctx.font = '30px sans-serif';


        ctx.textAlign = 'center';


        ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 10);


    }





    /* ----- public API ----- */


    window.game = {


        w,


        h,


        reset,


        tick,


        setDir,


        setFood,


        snake: () => snake.map(p => [p.x, p.y]),


        food: () => foodPos ? [foodPos.x, foodPos.y] : null,


        alive: () => alive,


        score: () => score,


    };





    function reset() {


        const cx = Math.floor(w / 2);


        const cy = Math.floor(h / 2);


        snake = [


            { x: cx + 1, y: cy },


            { x: cx,     y: cy },


            { x: cx - 1, y: cy }


        ];


        dir = { x: 1, y: 0 };


        nextDir = { x: 1, y: 0 };


        alive = true;


        score = 0;


        placeFood();


        draw();


    }





    function tick() {


        if (!alive) return;





        // apply queued direction (already validated in setDir)


        if (!(nextDir.x === -dir.x && nextDir.y === -dir.y)) {


            dir = nextDir;


        }





        const newHead = { x: snake[0].x + dir.x, y: snake[0].y + dir.y };





        // wall collision


        if (newHead.x < 0 || newHead.x >= w || newHead.y < 0 || newHead.y >= h) {


            alive = false;


            drawGameOver();


            return;


        }





        // self collision


        if (snake.some(p => p.x === newHead.x && p.y === newHead.y)) {


            alive = false;


            drawGameOver();


            return;


        }





        // move forward


        snake.unshift(newHead);





        // eat food?


        if (foodPos && newHead.x === foodPos.x && newHead.y === foodPos.y) {


            ++score;


            placeFood(); // grow (do not pop tail)


        } else {


            snake.pop(); // normal move


        }





        draw();


    }





    function setDir(direction) {


        const dirs = {


            up:    { x: 0, y: -

  function placeFoodRandom() {

  (function() {


    // ---------- Board Settings ----------


    const CELL_SIZE = 20;


    const GRID_W = 20;      // default width in cells (≥10)


    const GRID_H = 20;      // default height in cells (≥10)


    const CANVAS_WIDTH = GRID_W * CELL_SIZE;   // 400px


    const CANVAS_HEIGHT = GRID_H * CELL_SIZE;  // 400px





    // ---------- DOM Elements ----------


    const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


    const scoreDisplay = document.getElementById('score');


    const startBtn = document.getElementById('start');


    const pauseBtn = document.getElementById('pause');


    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset');





    // Set canvas dimensions (CSS + drawing buffer)


    canvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;


    canvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;


    canvas.style.width = CANVAS_WIDTH + 'px';


    canvas.style.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT + 'px';





    // ---------- Game State ----------


    let gameState = {


      snake: [],


      direction: 'right',


      nextDir: null,


      food: [0, 0],


      alive: true,


      score: 0,


      grow: false,


      intervalId: null,


      running: false


    };





    // ---------- Helper Functions ----------


    function isOpposite(dir1, dir2) {


      const opp = { up: 'down', down: 'up', left: 'right', right: 'left' };


      return opp[dir1] === dir2;


    }





    // Get all cells not occupied by the snake


    function getFreeCells() {


      const occupied = new Set(gameState.snake.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`));


      const free = [];


      for (let x = 0; x < GRID_W; x++) {


        for (let y = 0; y < GRID_H; y++) {


          if (!occupied.has(`${x},${y}`)) {


            free.push([x, y]);


          }


        }


      }


      return free;


    }





    // Place food randomly on a free cell


    function placeRandomFood() {


      const free = getFreeCells();


      if (free.length > 0) {


        const idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length);


        gameState.food = free[idx];


      } else {


        // No free cells (snake filled board) – place something anyway (shouldn't happen)


        gameState.food = [0, 0];


      }


    }





    // ---------- Drawing ----------


    function redraw() {


      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);





      // Draw grid background (optional: light borders)


      ctx.fillStyle = '#1a1a1a';


      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);





      // Draw snake


      gameState.snake.forEach(([x, y], index) => {


        // Head is slightly brighter


        ctx.fillStyle = index === 0 ? '#4caf50' : '#2e7d32';


        ctx.fillRect(x * CELL_SIZE, y * CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE);


        // Slight border


        ctx.strokeStyle = '#111';


        ctx.lineWidth = 1;


        ctx.strokeRect(x * CELL_SIZE, y * CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE);


      });





      // Draw food


      const [fx, fy] = gameState.food;


      ctx.fillStyle = '#e53935';


      ctx.fillRect(fx * CELL_SIZE, fy * CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE);


      ctx.strokeStyle = '#b71c1c';


      ctx.strokeRect(fx * CELL_SIZE, fy * CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE, CELL_SIZE);


    }





    function updateScoreDisplay() {


      scoreDisplay.textContent = 'Score: ' + gameState.score;


    }





    // ---------- Game Logic ----------


    function reset() {


      // Stop any auto-advance


      if (gameState.intervalId) {


        clearInterval(gameState.intervalId);


        gameState.intervalId = null;


        gameState.running = false;


      }





      // Initial snake: length 3, moving right, placed around middle


      const startX = Math.floor(GRID_W / 2);


      const startY = Math.floor(GRID_H / 2);


      gameState.snake = [


        [startX, startY],

<script>


(function(){


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const CELL = 20;


  const W = 20, H = 20;          // grid cells (>= 10), board = 400x400 CSS px


  canvas.width  = W * CELL;


  canvas.height = H * CELL;





  const DELTAS = { up:[0,-1], down:[0,1], left:[-1,0], right:[1,0] };


  const OPP    = { up:'down', down:'up', left:'right', right:'left' };





  let body, dir, queuedDir, food, alive, scoreVal;


  let loopId = null;


  const SPEED = 110; // ms per auto tick





  function placeFood(){


    const free = [];


    for (let y=0;y<H;y++) for (let x=0;x<W;x++){


      if (!body.some(s=>s[0]===x&&s[1]===y)) free.push([x,y]);


    }


    if (free.length===0){ food=null; return; }


    food = free[Math.floor(Math.random()*free.length)];


  }





  function resetState(){


    body = [[7,10],[6,10],[5,10]]; // head first, length 3


    dir = 'right';


    queuedDir = null;


    alive = true;


    scoreVal = 0;


    placeFood();


    updateHud();


    draw();


  }





  function setFood(x,y){


    if (typeof x!=='number'||typeof y!=='number') return;


    if (x<0||x>=W||y<0||y>=H) return;


    food = [x,y];


    draw();


  }





  function setDir(d){


    if (!alive) return;


    if (!DELTAS[d]) return;


    if (OPP[dir] === d) return;   // ignore 180° reversal vs current heading


    queuedDir = d;


  }





  function tick(){


    if (!alive) return;


    if (queuedDir){ dir = queuedDir; queuedDir = null; }


    const [dx,dy] = DELTAS[dir];


    const head = body[0];


    const nx = head[0]+dx, ny = head[1]+dy;





    // wall collision


    if (nx<0||nx>=W||ny<0||ny>=H){ alive=false; draw(); onDeath(); return; }





    const willEat = food && nx===food[0] && ny===food[1];


    // self collision (tail will move out unless eating)


    const checkBody = willEat ? body : body.slice(0, body.length-1);


    for (const s of checkBody){


      if (s[0]===nx && s[1]===ny){ alive=false; draw(); onDeath(); return; }


    }





    body.unshift([nx,ny]);


    if (willEat){ scoreVal++; placeFood(); updateHud(); }


    else { body.pop(); }


    draw();


  }





  function onDeath(){


    stopLoop();


    const st = document.getElementById('status');


    st.textContent = 'Game Over — press Reset';


    st.style.color = '#


…[truncated, 4401 more chars in transcript]

  <script>


    (function () {


      const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


      const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');





      const W = 20;


      const H = 20;


      const CELL = canvas.width / W; // 20





      const DIRS = {


        up: [0, -1],


        down: [0, 1],


        left: [-1, 0],


        right: [1, 0]


      };





      let snakeBody = [];


      let dir = [1, 0];


      let pendingDirs = [];


      let foodPos = [0, 0];


      let isAlive = false;


      let scoreVal = 0;


      let timer = null;


      const TICK_MS = 120;





      function opposite(a, b) {


        return a[0] === -b[0] && a[1] === -b[1];


      }





      function isOnSnake(pos) {


        for (let i = 0; i < snakeBody.length; i++) {


          if (snakeBody[i][0] === pos[0] && snakeBody[i][1] === pos[1]) return true;


        }


        return false;


      }





      function randomFood() {


        let pos;


        do {


          pos = [Math.floor(Math.random() * W), Math.floor(Math.random() * H)];


        } while (isOnSnake(pos));


        foodPos = pos;


      }





      function draw() {


        ctx.fillStyle = '#161b22';


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





        ctx.strokeStyle = '#21262d';


        ctx.lineWidth = 1;


        for (let i = 0; i <= W; i++) {


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.moveTo(i * CELL, 0);


          ctx.lineTo(i * CELL, canvas.height);


          ctx.stroke();


        }


        for (let j = 0; j <= H; j++) {


          ctx.beginPath();


          ctx.moveTo(0, j * CELL);


          ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, j * CELL);


          ctx.stroke();


        }





        ctx.fillStyle = '#f85149';


        ctx.beginPath();


        ctx.arc(


          foodPos[0] * CELL + CELL / 2,


          foodPos[1] * CELL + CELL / 2,


          CELL / 2 - 2,


          0,


          Math.PI * 2


        );


        ctx.fill();





        for (let i = 0; i < snakeBody.length; i++) {


          const [x, y] = snakeBody[i];


          if (i === 0) {


            ctx.fillStyle = '#2ea043';


          } else {


            ctx.fillStyle = '#238636';


          }


          ctx.fillRect(x * CELL + 1, y * CELL + 1, CELL - 2, CELL - 2);


        }


      }





      function updateScore() {


        scoreEl.textContent = 'Score: ' + scoreVal;


      }





      function tick() {


        if (!isAlive) return;





        while (pendingDirs.length > 0) {


          const d = pendingDirs.shift();


          const v = DIRS[d];


          if (!opposite(v, dir)) {


            dir = v;


            break;


          }


        }





        const head = snakeBody[0];


        const nx = head[0] + dir[0];


        const ny = head[1] + dir[1];


        const newHead = [nx, ny];





        snakeBody.unshift(newHead);





        if (nx === foodPos[0] && ny === foodPos[1]) {


          scoreVal++;


          randomFood();


        } else {


          snakeBody.pop();


        }





        if (nx < 0 || nx >= W || ny < 0 || ny >= H) {


          isAlive = false;


        } else {


          for (let i = 1; i < snakeBody.length; i++) {


            if (snakeBody[i][0] === nx && snakeBody[i][1] === ny) {


              isAlive = false;


              break;


            }


          }


        }





        updateScore();


        draw();





        if (!isAlive && timer) {


          clearInterval(timer);


          timer = null;


        }


      }





      function reset() {


        if (timer) {


          clearInterval(timer);


          timer = null;


        }


        const sx = Math.floor(W / 2);


        const sy = Math.floor(H / 2);


        snakeBody = [[sx, sy], [sx - 1, sy], [sx - 2, sy]];


        dir = [1, 0];


        pendingDirs = [];


        scoreVal = 0;


        isAlive = true;


        randomFood();

  const OPPOSITE = { up: 'down', down: 'up', left: 'right', right: 'left' };





  function cellKey(x, y) { return x + ',' + y; }





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const occupied = new Set(snakeBody.map(([x, y]) => cellKey(x, y)));


    const free = [];


    for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {


      for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {


        if (!occupied.has(cellKey(x, y))) free.push([x, y]);


      }


    }


    if (free.length === 0) return null;


    return free[(Math.random() * free.length) | 0];


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const cell = randomFreeCell();


    if (cell) foodPos = cell;


  }





  function reset() {

      [cx - 2, cy],

    pendingDir = null;


    isAlive = true;


    currentScore = 0;


    placeFood();


    updateScore();


    overlay.classList.remove('show');


    overlay.textContent = '';


    draw();


  }





  function updateScore() {


    scoreEl.textContent = String(currentScore);


  }





  function setDir(d) {


    if (!DIRS[d]) return;

    if (OPPOSITE[base] === d) return;


    pendingDir = d;

<script>


(function() {


  const COLS = 21, ROWS = 21;


  const CELL = 20; // 21*20 = 420px


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');


  const statusEl = document.getElementById('status');





  // Game state


  let snakeArr = [];


  let foodPos = [0, 0];


  let dir = 'right';


  let nextDir = 'right';


  let _score = 0;


  let _alive = false;


  let loopInterval = null;


  let running = false;





  const OPPOSITE = { up: 'down', down: 'up', left: 'right', right: 'left' };





  function initSnake() {


    // Start in middle, heading right, length 3


    const midY = Math.floor(ROWS / 2);


    const midX = Math.floor(COLS / 2);


    snakeArr = [


      [midX, midY],


      [midX - 1, midY],


      [midX - 2, midY]


    ];


    dir = 'right';


    nextDir = 'right';


  }





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const occupied = new Set(snakeArr.map(([x,y]) => x + ',' + y));


    const free = [];


    for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x++)


      for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++)


        if (!occupied.has(x + ',' + y)) free.push([x, y]);


    if (free.length === 0) return null;


    return free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const cell = randomFreeCell();


    if (cell) foodPos = cell;


  }





  function reset() {


    initSnake();


    _score = 0;


    _alive = true;


    scoreEl.textContent = '0';


    statusEl.textContent = 'Use arrow keys to steer.';


    placeFood();


    draw();


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!_alive) return;


    dir = nextDir;





    const head = snakeArr[0];


    let nx = head[0], ny = head[1];


    if (dir === 'up') ny--;


    else if (dir === 'down') ny++;


    else if (dir === 'left') nx--;


    else if (dir === 'right') nx++;





    // Wall collision


    if (nx < 0 || nx >= COLS || ny < 0 || ny >= ROWS) {


      _alive = false;


      statusEl.textContent = 'Game Over! Hit a wall.';


      draw();


      stopLoop();


      return;


    }





    // Self collision (exclude tail tip because it will move)


    for (let i = 0; i < snakeArr.length - 1; i++) {


      if (snakeArr[i][0] === nx && snakeArr[i][1] === ny) {


        _alive = false;


        statusEl.textContent = 'Game Over! Hit yourself.';


        draw();


        stopLoop();


        return;


      }


    }





    const ate = (nx === foodPos[0] && ny === foodPos[1]);


    snakeArr.unshift([nx, ny]);


    if (ate) {


      _score++;


      scoreEl.textContent = _score;


      placeFood();


    } else {


      snakeArr.pop();


    }


    draw();


  }





  function setDir(d) {


    if (!_alive) return;


    if (d === OPPOSITE[dir]) return; // ignore 180°


    nextDir = d;


  }





  function setFood(x, y) {


    foodPos = [x, y];


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    // Background grid


    ctx.fillStyle = '#16213e';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


    ctx.strokeStyle = '#1e2d50';


    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;


    for (let x = 0; x <= COLS; x++) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(x * CELL, 0);


      ctx.lineTo(x * CELL, ROWS * CELL);


      ctx.stroke();


    }


    for (let y = 0; y <= ROWS; y++) {


      ctx.beginPath();


      ctx.moveTo(0, y * CELL);


      ctx.lineTo(COLS * CELL, y * CELL);


      ctx.stroke();


    }





    // Food


    const fx = foodPos[0] * CELL,

(function(){


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');


  const statusEl = document.getElementById('status');





  const W = 20, H = 20; // grid cells, >=10

    down:  {x:0, y:1},

    arr = arr || snakeArr;


    for(let i=0;i<arr.length;i++){


      if(arr[i][0]===x && arr[i][1]===y) return true;


    }


    return false;

  function placeFood(){


    let x,y;


    let attempts = 0;


    do{


      x = randInt(W);


      y = randInt(H);


      attempts++;


    } while(isOnSnake(x,y) && attempts < 1000);

    if(isOnSnake(nx, ny, bodyCheck)){


      aliveFlag = false;


      onDeath();


      return;

(function() {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');


  const msgEl = document.getElementById('message');





  const COLS = 21;


  const ROWS = 21;


  const CELL = Math.floor(canvas.width / COLS);





  // Effective canvas size based on cell size


  const CW = COLS * CELL;


  const CH = ROWS * CELL;





  let snakeBody, dir, nextDir, foodPos, _score, _alive;


  let loopId = null;


  let paused = false;


  const TICK_MS = 120;





  function initState() {


    const midX = Math.floor(COLS / 2);


    const midY = Math.floor(ROWS / 2);


    snakeBody = [


      [midX, midY],


      [midX - 1, midY],


      [midX - 2, midY],


    ];


    dir = 'right';


    nextDir = 'right';


    _score = 0;


    _alive = true;


    placeFood();


    scoreEl.textContent = '0';


    msgEl.textContent = '';


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const free = [];


    const occupied = new Set(snakeBody.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`));


    for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x++) {


      for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {


        if (!occupied.has(`${x},${y}`)) free.push([x, y]);


      }


    }


    if (free.length === 0) return;


    foodPos = free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function step() {


    if (!_alive) return;





    dir = nextDir;


    const [hx, hy] = snakeBody[0];


    let nx = hx, ny = hy;


    if (dir === 'up')    ny--;


    if (dir === 'down')  ny++;


    if (dir === 'left')  nx--;


    if (dir === 'right') nx++;





    // Wall collision


    if (nx < 0 || nx >= COLS || ny < 0 || ny >= ROWS) {


      _alive = false;


      draw();


      msgEl.textContent = 'GAME OVER — hit a wall';


      stopLoop();


      return;


    }





    // Self collision (exclude tail tip which will move away, unless eating)


    const eating = (nx === foodPos[0] && ny === foodPos[1]);


    const checkBody = eating ? snakeBody : snakeBody.slice(0, -1);


    for (const [bx, by] of checkBody) {


      if (nx === bx && ny === by) {


        _alive = false;


        draw();


        msgEl.textContent = 'GAME OVER — hit yourself';


        stopLoop();


        return;


      }


    }





    snakeBody.unshift([nx, ny]);


    if (eating) {


      _score++;


      scoreEl.textContent = _score;


      placeFood();


    } else {


      snakeBody.pop();


    }


    draw();


  }

  const COLOR_BODY    = '#27a85a';

  const COLOR_FOOD_GLOW = '#ff4e4e44';


  const COLOR_GRID    = '#161616';


  const COLOR_DEAD    = '#555';

<script>


(function () {


  const W = 20, H = 20;             // grid cells


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.querySelector('#score b');


  const statusEl = document.getElementById('status');





  const CELL = canvas.width / W;   // pixels per cell





  let snakeArr, foodPos, dir, pendingDir, aliveFlag, scoreVal;


  let loopId = null;


  const SPEED = 110; // ms per tick





  function reset() {


    const cx = Math.floor(W / 2), cy = Math.floor(H / 2);


    // head first


    snakeArr = [


      [cx, cy],


      [cx - 1, cy],


      [cx - 2, cy]


    ];


    dir = 'right';


    pendingDir = 'right';


    aliveFlag = true;


    scoreVal = 0;


    placeFood();


    updateScore();


    setStatus('Ready');


    draw();


  }





  function updateScore() { scoreEl.textContent = scoreVal; }





  function setStatus(msg, dead) {


    statusEl.textContent = msg;


    statusEl.classList.toggle('dead', !!dead);


  }





  function occupied(x, y) {


    return snakeArr.some(s => s[0] === x && s[1] === y);


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const free = [];


    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++)


      for (let y = 0; y < H; y++)


        if (!occupied(x, y)) free.push([x, y]);


    if (free.length === 0) { foodPos = null; return; }


    foodPos = free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function setFood(x, y) {


    foodPos = [x | 0, y | 0];


    draw();


  }





  const OPP = { up: 'down', down: 'up', left: 'right', right: 'left' };


  const DELTA = {


    up: [0, -1], down: [0, 1], left: [-1, 0], right: [1, 0]


  };





  function setDir(d) {


    if (!DELTA[d]) return;


    // ignore 180 reversal relative to current heading


    if (OPP[d] === dir) return;


    pendingDir = d;


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!aliveFlag) return;


    dir = pendingDir;


    const [dx, dy] = DELTA[dir];


    const head = snakeArr[0];


    const nx = head[0] + dx;


    const ny = head[1] + dy;





    // wall collision


    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= W || ny >= H) {


      die();


      return;


    }





    const eating = foodPos && nx === foodPos[0] && ny === foodPos[1];





    // body collision: check against all cells except the tail that will move,


    // unless we're eating (tail stays).


    const bodyToCheck = eating ? snakeArr : snakeArr.slice(0, snakeArr.length - 1);


    if (bodyToCheck.some(s => s[0] === nx && s[1] === ny)) {


      die();


      return;


    }





    snakeArr.unshift([nx, ny]);


    if (eating) {


      scoreVal += 1;


      updateScore();


      placeFood();


    } else {


      snakeArr.pop();


    }


    draw();


  }





  function die() {


    aliveFlag = false;


    stopLoop();


    setStatus('Game Over — Score ' + scoreVal, true);


    draw();


  }





  function draw() {


    // background


    ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0e14';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.

  };





  function cellsEqual(a, b) { return a[0] === b[0] && a[1] === b[1]; }





  function occupied(x, y) {


    for (let i = 0; i < snakeCells.length; i++) {


      if (snakeCells[i][0] === x && snakeCells[i][1] === y) return true;


    }


    return false;


  }





  function placeFoodRandom() {

    for (let y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {


      for (let x = 0; x < COLS; x++) {

      }


    }


    if (free.length === 0) { foodCell = null; return; }

    }


    return false;


  }





  function placeFood(){


    var free = [];


    for (var y = 0; y < H; y++){


      for (var x = 0; x < W; x++){


        if (!occupied(x, y)) free.push([x, y]);


      }


    }


    if (!free.length){ food = null; return; }


    food = free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)].slice();


  }





  function reset(){


    var cy = Math.floor(H / 2), cx = Math.floor(W / 2);

    dir = 'right';


    nextDir = 'right';


    alive = true;


    score = 0;

    updateHud();

(function () {


  'use strict';





  var COLS = 20, ROWS = 20;      // grid size in cells (>= 10x10)


  var CELL = 22;                 // css px per cell


  var SIZE = COLS * CELL;        // 440 css px board





  var canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  var dpr = Math.max(1, Math.min(3, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));


  canvas.style.width = SIZE + 'px';


  canvas.style.height = SIZE + 'px';


  canvas.width = SIZE * dpr;


  canvas.height = SIZE * dpr;


  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);





  var scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');


  var statusEl = document.getElementById('status');


  var startBtn = document.getElementById('start');


  var pauseBtn = document.getElementById('pause');


  var resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset');





  var DIRS = {


    up: [0, -1],


    down: [0, 1],


    left: [-1, 0],


    right: [1, 0]


  };





  var snakeArr, dirName, pendingName, foodPos, scoreVal, isAlive, won;


  var timer = null;





  function opposite(a, b) {


    return DIRS[a][0] + DIRS[b][0] === 0 && DIRS[a][1] + DIRS[b][1] === 0;


  }





  function setStatus(t) { statusEl.textContent = t; }


  function updateScore() { scoreEl.textContent = String(scoreVal); }





  function freeCells() {


    var occ = {};


    for (var i = 0; i < snakeArr.length; i++) occ[snakeArr[i][0] + ',' + snakeArr[i][1]] = true;


    var cells = [];


    for (var x = 0; x < COLS; x++) {


      for (var y = 0; y < ROWS; y++) {


        if (!occ[x + ',' + y]) cells.push([x, y]);


      }


    }


    return cells;


  }





  function placeFood() {


    var cells = freeCells();


    if (cells.length === 0) { foodPos = [-1, -1]; won = true; return; }


    foodPos = cells[Math.floor(Math.random() * cells.length)];


  }





  function reset() {


    stopTimer();


    var cx = Math.floor(COLS / 2), cy = Math.floor(ROWS / 2);


    snakeArr = [[cx, cy], [cx - 1, cy], [cx - 2, cy]]; // head first, length 3


    dirName = 'right';


    pendingName = 'right';


    scoreVal = 0;


    isAlive = true;


    won = false;


    placeFood();

(function () {

    down:  { x: 0,  y: 1  },


    left:  { x: -1, y: 0  },


    right: { x: 1,  y: 0  },


  };


  const OPPOSITE = { up: "down", down: "up", left: "right", right: "left" };





  let snakeBody;   // array of [x, y], head first


  let dir;         // current heading, applied last tick


  let dirQueue;    // pending turns


  let foodPos;     // [x, y]


  let isAlive;


  let currentScore;


  let timer = null;


  const TICK_MS = 130;





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const occupied = new Set(snakeBody.map(([x, y]) => x + "," + y));


    const free = [];


    for (let y = 0; y < H; y++)


      for (let x = 0; x < W; x++)


        if (!occupied.has(x + "," + y)) free.push([x, y]);


    if (free.length === 0) return null;


    return free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function reset() {


    stopLoop();


    const cy = Math.floor(H / 2);


    const cx = Math.floor(W / 2);


    snakeBody = [[cx, cy], [cx - 1, cy], [cx - 2, cy]];


    dir = "right";


    dirQueue = [];


    isAlive = true;


    currentScore = 0;


    foodPos = randomFreeCell();


    updateScore();


    showOverlay("Ready", "Press Start or an arrow key");


    draw();


  }

  <script>


    // Game constants


    const GRID_SIZE = 20;


    const CELL_SIZE = 400 / GRID_SIZE;


    


    // Create canvas and context references


    const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


    const startBtn = document.getElementById('start');


    const pauseBtn = document.getElementById('pause');


    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset');


    const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');





    // Game state


    let snake = [];


    let food = [0, 0];


    let direction = 'right';


    let nextDirection = 'right';


    let gameInterval = null;


    let isRunning = false;


    let isPaused = false;


    let score = 0;


    let gameOver = false;





    // Initialize the game


    function initGame() {


      snake = [[5, 10], [4, 10], [3, 10]]; // Start with length 3


      direction = 'right';


      nextDirection = 'right';


      score = 0;


      gameOver = false;


      updateScore();


      placeFood();


      draw();


    }





    function startGame() {


      if (!gameInterval) {


        isRunning = true;


        isPaused = false;


        gameInterval = setInterval(gameTick, 150); // ~6.7 FPS


      } else if (isPaused) {


        isPaused = false;


        draw();


      }


    }





    function pauseGame() {


      if (gameInterval && !isPaused) {


        isPaused = true;


        clearInterval(gameInterval);


        gameInterval = null;


        // Draw paused overlay


        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)';


        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);


        


        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';


        ctx.font = '30px Arial';


        ctx.textAlign = 'center';


        ctx.fillText("PAUSED", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 15);


        ctx.font = '16px Arial';


        ctx.fillText("Press Start to resume", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 15);


      }


    }





    function resetGame() {


      clearInterval(gameInterval);


      gameInterval = null;


      isRunning = false;


      isPaused = false;


      gameOver = false;


      initGame();


    }





    // Game tick logic


    function gameTick() {


      if (isPaused) return;





      // Update direction from nextDirection to prevent 180-degree turns in one tick


      direction = nextDirection;





      // Calculate new head position


      const [headX, headY] = snake[0];


      let newX = headX;


      let newY = headY;





      switch (direction) {


        case 'up':    newY -= 1; break;


        case 'down':  newY += 1; break;


        case 'left':  newX -= 1; break;


        case 'right': newX += 1; break;


      }





      // Check for collisions


      if (newX < 0 || newX >= GRID_SIZE || newY < 0 || newY >= GRID_SIZE) {


        gameOver = true;


        endGame();

        function getDirectionVector(dir) {

                'up': [0, -1],


                'down': [0, 1],


                'left': [-1, 0],


                'right': [1, 0]


            };


            return vectors[dir];


        }





        function isOpposite(dir1, dir2) {


            const opposites = {


                'up': 'down',


                'down': 'up',


                'left': 'right',


                'right': 'left'


            };


            return opposites[dir1] === dir2;


        }





        function setDir(newDir) {


            if (!isOpposite(direction, newDir)) {


                nextDirection = newDir;


            }

        function tick() {

<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>

<style>


  body{margin:0;background:#222;color:#eee;font-family:sans-serif;text-align:center;}

<canvas id="snake" width="400" height="400"></canvas>





<div id="controls">


  <button id="start">Start</button>


  <button id="pause">Pause</button>


  <button id="reset">Reset</button>


</div>





<div id="score">Score: 0</div>





<script>


(() => {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');





  // Grid size (cells)


  const w = 20; // columns


  const h = 20; // rows





  const cellSize = Math.min(canvas.width / w, canvas.height / h);





  let snakeBody = []; // array of {x,y}


  let direction = 'right'; // current dir


  let nextDir = null; // queued turn


  let food = null;


  let alive = true;


  let score = 0;





  let intervalId = null;


  const tickRate = 200; // ms per tick





  function init() {


    snakeBody = [{x: Math.floor(w/2)-1, y: Math.floor(h/2)},


                 {x: Math.floor(w/2),     y: Math.floor(h/2)},


                 {x: Math.floor(w/2)+1,   y: Math.floor(h/2)}];


    direction = 'right';


    nextDir = null;


    alive = true;


    score = 0;


    placeFood();


    draw();


    updateScore();


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const free = [];


    for (let x=0;x<w;x++){


      for(let y=0;y<h;y++){


        if (!snakeBody.some(s=>s.x===x && s.y===y)) free.push({x,y});


      }


    }


    food = free[Math.floor(Math.random()*free.length)];


  }





  function setFood(x, y) {


    if (x>=0&&x<w&&y>=0&&y<h&&!snakeBody.some(s=>s.x===x&&s.y===y)){


      food={x,y};


      draw();


    }


  }





  function setDir(dir){


    const opposites = {up:'down', down:'up', left:'right', right:'left'};


    if (!alive) return;


    if (dir===opposites[direction]) return; // ignore reverse


    nextDir = dir;


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!alive) return;





    if (nextDir){


      direction = nextDir;


      nextDir = null;


    }


    const head = {x: snakeBody[0].x, y: snakeBody[0].y};


    switch(direction){


      case 'up': head.y--; break;


      case 'down': head.y++; break;


      case 'left': head.x--; break;


      case 'right': head.x++; break;


    }





    // Wall collision


    if (head.x<0||head.x>=w||head.y<0||head.y>=h){


      alive = false; draw(); return;


    }


    // Self collision


    if (snakeBody.some(s=>s.x===head.x&&s.y===head.y)){


      alive = false; draw(); return;


    }





    snakeBody.unshift(head);





    // Food eaten?


    if (food && head.x===food.x && head.y===food.y){


      score++;


      placeFood();


    } else {


      snakeBody.pop(); // move forward


    }


    draw();


    updateScore();


  }





  function draw(){


    ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);


    // Draw food


    if (food){


      ctx.fillStyle='#f00';


      ctx.fillRect(food.x*cellSize, food.y*cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);


    }


    // Draw snake


    for(let i=0;i<snakeBody.length;i++){


      const s = snakeBody[i];


      ctx.fillStyle=i===0?'#0f0':'#8f8';


      ctx.fillRect(s.x*cellSize, s.y*cellSize, cellSize, cellSize);


    }


    // If dead, overlay


    if (!alive){


      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.5)';


      ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);


      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';


      ctx.font='48px sans-serif';


      ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';


      ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2);


    }


  }





  function updateScore(){


    scoreEl.textContent = 'Score: '+score;


  }





  // Auto play controls


  document.getElementById('start').onclick=()=>{if(intervalId)return;intervalId=setInterval(tick,tickRate);}


  document.getElementById('pause').onclick=()=>{clearInterval(intervalId);intervalId=null;}


  document.getElementById('reset').onclick=()=>{init();}





  // Keyboard


  window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{


    if(e.key==='ArrowUp') setDir('up');


    else if(e.key==='ArrowDown') setDir('down');


    else if(e.key==='ArrowLeft') setDir('left');


    else if(e.key==='ArrowRight') setDir('right');


  });





  // Expose API


  window.game = {


    w, h,


    reset: init,


    tick,


    setDir,


    setFood,


    snake: () => snakeBody.map(s=>[s.x,s.y]),


    food: () => [food.x, food.y],


    alive: () => alive,


    score: () => score


  };





  // Start with a fresh game


  init();


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>

<script>


(function () {


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');





  // --- grid config (≥10×10) ---


  const GRID = 20;               // cells across/down


  const CELL = canvas.width / GRID;   // 20 px per cell





  let snake_, dir_, nextDir_, food_, alive_, score_, w_, h_, interval_;





  function init() {


    w_ = GRID;


    h_ = GRID;


    // snake starts at centre, length 3, heading right: head is last element


    const cx = Math.floor(w_ / 2);


    const cy = Math.floor(h_ / 2);


    snake_ = [


      [cx, cy],        // tail


      [cx - 1, cy],


      [cx - 2, cy]     // head


    ];


    dir_ = 'right';


    nextDir_ = null;            // pending turn (processed on tick)


    food_ = placeFood();


    alive_ = true;


    score_ = 0;


    scoreEl.textContent = 'Score: 0';


  }





  function freeCells() {


    const occupied = new Set(snake_.map(s => s[0] + ',' + s[1]));


    const cells = [];


    for (let x = 0; x < w_; x++)


      for (let y = 0; y < h_; y++)


        if (!occupied.has(x + ',' + y)) cells.push([x, y]);


    return cells;


  }





  function placeFood() {


    const free = freeCells();


    if (free.length === 0) return [0, 0]; // shouldn't happen while alive


    return free[Math.floor(Math.random() * free.length)];


  }





  function opposite(a, b) {


    return (a === 'up' && b === 'down') ||


           (a === 'down' && b === 'up') ||


           (a === 'left' && b === 'right') ||


           (a === 'right' && b === 'left');


  }





  function head() { return snake_[snake_.length - 1]; }





  function tick() {


    if (!alive_) return;





    // Apply queued direction (ignore 180° reversal)


    if (nextDir_ !== null && !opposite(nextDir_, dir_)) {


      dir_ = nextDir_;


    }


    nextDir_ = null;





    const [hx, hy] = head();


    let nx = hx, ny = hy;


    if (dir_ === 'up')    ny--;


    if (dir_ === 'down')  ny++;


    if (dir_ === 'left')  nx--;


    if (dir_ === 'right') nx++;





    // Wall collision


    if (nx < 0 || nx >= w_ || ny < 0 || ny >= h_) {


      alive_ = false;


      render();


      return;


    }





    const newHead = [nx, ny];





    // Self collision (skip the tail because it will move away — unless we grow)


    let grows = (nx === food_[0] && ny === food_[1]);


    // Check collision with all segments except possibly the last tail cell


    const limit = grows ? snake_.length - 1 : snake_.length;


    for (let i = 0; i < limit; i++) {


      if (snake_[i][0] === nx && snake_[i][1] === ny) {


        alive_ = false;


        render();


        return;


      }


    }





    snake_.push(newHead);


    if (grows) {


      score_++;


      scoreEl.textContent = 'Score: ' + score_;


      food_ = placeFood();


    } else {


      snake_.shift();


    }





    render();


  }





  // --- drawing ---


  function render() {


    // Background


    ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0f1a';


    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    // Grid lines (subtle)


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.03)';


    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;


    for (let i = 0; i <= GRID; i++) {


      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i * CE

<script>


(function() {


    // Configuration


    const CELL_SIZE = 20;


    const GRID_W = 400 / CELL_SIZE; // 20 cells (>=10)


    const GRID_H = 400 / CELL_SIZE; // 20 cells





    let snakeArr = [];   // Array of objects {x, y}. Index 0 is HEAD.


    let foodPos = null;  // Object {x, y}


    


    let currentDir = 'right'; // Direction currently moving in the grid (applied)


    let nextDirBuffer = 'right'; // Input to apply on next tick





    let scoreVal = 0;


    let isAlive = true;


    let gameLoopId = null;





    const canvas = document.getElementById('snake');


    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


    const scoreEl = document.getElementById('score');





    function initGame() {


        // Snake starts length >= 3. Start centered, moving right.


        snakeArr = [


            { x: 10, y: 10 }, // HEAD (index 0)


            { x: 9, y: 10 }, 


            { x: 8, y: 10 }   // TAIL (last index)


        ];





        scoreVal = 0;


        isAlive = true;


        currentDir = 'right';


        nextDirBuffer = 'right';





        spawnFood();


        draw(); 


    }





    function spawnFood() {


        let valid = false;


        while (!valid) {


            const fx = Math.floor(Math.random() * GRID_W);


            const fy = Math.floor(Math.random() * GRID_H);





            // Check if food overlaps with any part of the snake

            createCell(x, y);

        function checkCollision() {


            if (snake.some(([x, y]) => x < 0 || x >= w || y < 0 || y >= h)) {


                return true;


            }

                const [x, y] = cell;

            })) {

            }

                    break;


                case 'down':


                    newHead = [headX + 1, headY];


                    break;


                case 'left':


                    newHead = [headX, headY - 1];


                    break;


                case 'right':


                    newHead = [headX, headY + 1];


                    break;


            }

                clearInterval(gameInterval);


                return;


            }

            } else {


                snake.pop();


            }





            drawSnake();


            drawFood();


        }

(function () {


  // --- Config ---


  const CELL = 40;


  const W = 18, H = 18;


  const TICK_MS = 120;





  // --- Canvas ---


  const canvas = document.getElementById('snake-board');


  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');


  canvas.width = CELL * W;


  canvas.height = CELL * H;





  // --- State ---


  let snake, dir, nextDir, food, score, alive, running;





  function initState() {


    const cx = Math.floor(W / 2), cy = Math.floor(H / 2);


    snake = [


      [cx - 1, cy],


      [cx - 2, cy],


      [cx - 3, cy]


    ];


    dir = 'right'; nextDir = 'right';


    score = 0; alive = true; running = false;


  }





  function randomFreeCell() {


    const taken = new Set(snake.map(s => s[0] + ',' + s[1]));


    let x, y, attempts = W * H;


    while (attempts--) {


      x = Math.floor(Math.random() * W);


      y = Math.floor(Math.random() * H);


      if (!taken.has(x + ',' + y)) return [x, y];


    }


    // Fallback: scan first empty cell


    for (let i = 0; i < snake.length; i++) {


      const s = snake[i];


      for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {


        for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {


          if (x + ',' + y !== s[0] + ',' + s[1]) return [x, y];


        }


      }


    }


    return [0, 0];


  }





  function placeFood() { food = randomFreeCell(); }





  // --- Direction queue (ignore 180°) ---


  const DIRS = { up: { x: 0, y: -1 }, down: { x: 0, y: 1 }, left: { x: -1, y: 0 }, right: { x: 1, y: 0 } };





  function setDir(d) {


    if (!alive || !running) return;


    const current = DIRS[dir];


    const reverse = DIRS[d] && (DIRS[d].x === -current.x && DIRS[d].y === -current.y);


    if (!reverse) nextDir = d;


  }





  function tick() {


    if (!alive || !running) return;


    dir = nextDir;


    const head = snake[0];


    const nv = DIRS[dir];


    const nx = head[0] + nv.x, ny = head[1] + nv.y;





    // Wall collision


    if (nx < 0 || nx >= W || ny < 0 || ny >= H) { alive = false; return; }





    // Self collision


    for (let i = 0; i < snake.length; i++) {


      if (snake[i][0] === nx && snake[i][1] === ny) { alive = false; return; }


    }

    if (nx === food[0] && ny === food[1]) {


      score++; ate = true; placeFood();


    }


    snake.unshift(newHead);


    if (!ate) snake.pop();


  }





  // --- Rendering ---


  function draw() {


    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);





    // Grid lines (subtle)

    ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;


    for (let x = 1; x < W; x++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x * CELL, 0); ctx.lineTo(x * CELL, H * CELL); ctx.stroke(); }


    for (let y = 1; y < H; y++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y * CELL); ctx.lineTo(W * CELL, y * CELL); ctx.stroke(); }

    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff5252';

    ctx.shadowBlur = 8;


    ctx.beginPath();

    ctx.fill();


    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;





    // Snake


    snake.forEach((seg, i) => {


      const x = seg[0] * CELL + CELL / 2, y = seg[1] * CELL + CELL / 2;


      const isHead = i === 0;


      ctx.fillStyle = isHead ? '#4caf50' : '#66bb6a';

    const container = document.createElement('div');


    container.style.width = canvas.width + 'px';


    container.style.height = canvas.height + 'px';


    container.style.position = 'absolute';


    container.style.left = window.screenX - container.offsetWidth / 2 + 'px';


    container.style.top = window.screenY - container.offsetHeight / 2 + 'px';


    document.body.appendChild(container);





    // Set transform to center the canvas on screen (x,y) so that it looks centered at (0,0)

    const centerX = rect.width / 2;


    const centerY = rect.height / 2;

    // Draw walls: vertical lines from x=leftWall to rightWall, y=topWall to bottomWall


    ctx.strokeStyle = 'black';

        ctx.beginPath();


        ctx.moveTo(x1, y);


        ctx.lineTo(x2, y);


        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.lineTo(x2, y);

    }

Claude has no cost figure here, on purpose. Every Claude model is measured through the Claude Code CLI, which runs on a subscription — there is no per-token price to report, so any number would be invented. Every other row is what the provider billed, or list pricing on real token counts. We tried publishing a derived API-equivalent and measurement refuted it twice: the CLI sends its own instructions, reads its own files and carries its own conversation, so it consumed 16× more input per turn than the same model doing the same task through an API — a different agent, not a fixed overhead, and nothing you can subtract. Claude cost lands here when the full suite has been run through the API, not before.
Latest-per-model table ranks by score, then cost, then speed. Token counts come from provider usage fields. Outputs are verbatim transcripts, truncated for display — full text lives in runs/…/transcript.jsonl.
